# Frozen test catalog

All paths are relative to the public pipeline repository. This generated catalog inventories records and source locations; it does not execute or certify their mathematics. The companion PDF supplies the interpreted claim-to-test route.

The `bound_to_selected_route` marker is a transitive **binding-reference** closure, including provenance and controls. It is not a minimal execution schedule or a claim that every referenced record supplies a mathematical premise. Failed and superseded records are retained.

The JSON companion includes every resolved input/hash, static gate expression and line number, saved boolean, entry source, output and dependency. Gate extraction is syntactic; data-dependent labels and unrecognized helper calls require reading the source.

## 20261026_GROWING_MODE_CUBIC_WALL

Prospective neutral growing-mode cubic wall derivation ==================================================== No calculation has been executed by the author of this file. The exact polynomial qualifier in wall.py must be frozen and run on Linux. Convention: eta=epsilon exp(r t), ordinary coefficients, r>h>0; both conserved currents vanish. First-order tracefree and Israel equations give b1=-2p1 and xi1=0. Fields p_j,f_j,b_j are Eulerian; chi_j is intrinsic to the wall. Set x=xi2, p=p1, P=p2, c=c2, f=f1, F=f2, g=f1', G=f2', u=p1', gp=f1'', chi=chi1 and chi2=the second wall oscillator coefficient. Use sigma=+1 at the lower wall and sigma=-1 at the upper wall. The wall shift is eta^2 x+eta^3 xi3. Consequently log(a(S)/a_i) = eta^2 Hx+eta^3 Hxi3, p(S) = eta p+eta^2 P+eta^3(p3+xu), b(S) = -2eta p+eta^2(c-2P)+eta^3(c3-2p3-2xu), deltaPhi(S) = eta f+eta^2(F+Tx)+eta^3(f3+Txi3+xg). Velocity begins at eta^2, so all boost corrections begin at eta^4 and cannot contribute to the displayed cubic rows. 

**Folder:** `data/raw/l001/experiments/20261026_GROWING_MODE_CUBIC_WALL`

**Contracts:** `data/raw/l001/experiments/20261026_GROWING_MODE_CUBIC_WALL/DERIVATION.txt`

## 20261026_GROWING_NEUTRAL_METRIC_RESPONSE

Compatible all-neutral-order real-axis reconstruction ==================================================== For n>=2 and mu=-nr(nr+3h), put D=2T^2-3(mu+4k)E^2, J=D-2T^2, B=12HT-4mPhi. The homogeneous radial operator is supplemented by compatible sources: p'=-2Hp-Tf/3+Sp, f'=g, g'=-4Hg+(m-mu E^2+2T^2)f+Bp+Sg, Tg-T'f+Dp=Sc. Reconstruct p=(Sc-Tf'+T'f)/D. Eliminating it gives f''+(4H+BT/D)f'-(m-mu E^2+2T^2+BT'/D)f = Sg+BSc/D. Differentiating the reconstructed p and using the reduced scalar equation gives, exactly, p'-(-2Hp-Tf/3+Sp)=(Sc'+4HSc-TSg-DSp)/D. The identity is qualified by clearing D^2 in an exact sparse polynomial ring with a general mu; no special negative forced rate is substituted. Compatibility is a hypothesis on the source data, not an optional check. With sigma=+1 at the lower wall and -1 at the upper wall, the original Robin equation is 2sigma(f'+2Tp)-Gamma f=R, Gamma=21-1/(2-mu E^2). ## 20261026_GROWING_NEUTRAL_TRACE_WALL

Conditional all-order trace and wall closure, neutral growing family =================================================================== At a static background take the exact first variation with a general temporal rate lambda. The exact polynomial qualifier verifies the rows trace: lambda(lambda-h)c, graph: sigma E^2 lambda(lambda-h)xi, oscillator: [2+lambda(lambda+3h)E^2]chi-f-Txi, scalar normal: g+T'xi-Tc+2Tp-sigma[21(f+Txi)-chi]/2. It retains displacement, moving evaluation of the background scalar, and oscillator coupling. Velocity-square/boost effects have no linear term. Why this is the principal operator at every order, not an extrapolation from the quadratic and cubic results: expand in positive ordinary powers of eta with eta_t=r eta, fixed static zeroth-order data and zero currents. At order n, a new nth-order coefficient enters linearly, with derivative rate lambda=nr. Multiplying it by any positive-order perturbation gives order greater than n. Analytic inverse lapse/boost

These are bound improvements, not observed solution amplitudes or nonlinear radius gains. For BOTH independent engines, the largest old summed estimate and both new component bounds are controlled by the high-order range n>=65. ## 20261026_NEUTRAL_ANALYTIC_SEED

Analytic first-mode seed norm; same full intervals and selected neutral mode. Replay the exact fresh initializer and both validated radial engines, with unchanged steps 1/4096 and 1/8192, 768-bit Arb and all whole-cell original constraints. Record real M=max(|p1|,|f1|,|g1|) on every complete cell. The first mode obeys p'=-2Hp-Tf/3, f'=g, g'=-4Hg+(m-mu1 E^2+2T^2)f+(12HT-4mPhi)p. In the certified background collar, |H|<=3,|T|<=2,|Phi|<=2,|E|<=17, m<=1,r,h<=1/32, mu1=-r(r+3h), hence |mu1|<=1/256. Its matrix row sums are <=104. Along closest-real-point segments length rho, Gronwall and exp(x)<=1/(1-x) give Mcomplex<=M/(1-104rho), provided 104rho<1. This is an analytic first-mode extension of the selected real solution, not an inversion at the resonant first harmonic. The background derivative bounds |H'|<=2 and |T'|<=26 imply |p'|<=7 Mcomplex, |p''|<56 Mcomplex, |f'|<=Mcomplex, |f''|<=104 Mcomplex. The first-order trace is c1=0, b1=-2p1. No new inverse is asserted. Authenticated ANALYTIC_INVERSE/DERIVATION.txt section4 gives EACH bound n^2|f(real)|<=C0*S and n|f'(real)|<=C0*S, with S=||F||_(2n+2)+n max|g|. The exact C0 is in its RESULT.json. The full complex derivative norm U(f) uses the larger Cf (Cscalar). On the real interval the Nagumo weight d equals1. For n>=2, p=(Sc-Tf'+T'f)/D and D>=d0*n^2 imply n^2|p|<=[||Sc||+(Treal/2+Tpreal/4)*C0*S]/d0. Using Treal<=1,Tpreal<=9 gives Cm=(11/4)/d0 and therefore n^2|p|<=Cm*(Sc+C0*S). Keeping an extraSp remains conservative. This uses only f and f' at the real anchor, not complex f''. Set Sbound=force+g, uf=Cf*Sbound, ureal=C0*Sbound. Change ONLY xp=[Cm*(sc+uf+sp)+rho*(uf/6+sp/2)]/(1-3rho/2) to xp=[Cm*(sc+ureal+sp)+rho*(uf/6+sp/2)]/(1-3rho/2). All complex continuation, yp,up,oscillator and rem

## 20261026_REAL_AXIS_OSCILLATOR_ENDPOINT

Real-axis oscillator endpoint estimate; same coupled certificate. Only the C response changes relative to REAL_AXIS_METRIC_ANCHOR_R1. The inherited neutral Q5 row is chi_n=(f_n+T_i*xi_n-O_n)/d_n, d_n=2+nr(nr+3h)E_i^2>=n^2/16384, using r>=1/64, E_i>=1/2, h>0 on the real background. Multiply by n^3, n>=2: n^3|chi_n|<=16384[n|f_n|+|T_i|n|xi_n|+n|O_n|]. The authenticated real scalar inverse supplies n^2|f_n(real)|<=C0*S. Hence n|f_n|<=C0*S/2. The summed graph response Xnew bounds n^3|xi_n| for either wall, hence n|xi_n|<=Xnew/4. Use the authenticated real |T_i|<=1 and the unchanged oscillator nonlinear load envelope o>=n|O_n|. Doubling the per-wall bound yields Cnew=32768(ureal/2+Xnew/4+o), ureal=C0*(force+g). The factor2 is conservative; it does not remove wall orientations or the graph displacement from the oscillator source. Replace uf/2 by ureal/2 ONLY in the final oscillator response. Keep the real metric anchor already corrected, every complex uf term, all source envelopes, lower-ord

## 20261027_BULK_DEPENDENCY_CERTIFICATE

Prospective explicit bulk-source dependency comparison. Same physical family, seed1215, joint denominator weights, 283 norm rays. Baseline replays every joint DENOMINATOR_CERTIFICATE row and full certificate. Changed lane: W=B+X instead of Y=B+X+C only for TRACE, BM, BK, BR. Exact pulled bulk equations and trace transport depend on bulk fields and graph displacement, not the independent wall oscillator explicitly. Preserve indirect oscillator feedback through solved bulk/graph fields. GRAPH retains its C-squared source; BJ and BO retain Y. Final contraction, chart, lapse and observable envelopes retain full aggregate Y=B+X+C. Subtract each lane's own constant and linear jets before comparing loads. Verify unchanged GRAPH/BJ/BO loads and non-increase of four changed loads, then all masses. Recompute both complete certificates on same grid. No physical oscillator decoupling claim. No new ODE or finite trajectory. Linux old lease; 600s cap; fixed cutoff1793074031 before/after admission. N

## 20261027_CANCELLATION_CONTOUR_CERTIFICATE

Same neutral family, fixed global R, no physical evolution. Window cutoff 2026-10-27T11:38:31Z. Preserve every existing acceptance gate. Use exact identity F/r=(eta D'-(D-D0))+eta D'*(J-1), J=1/ell. This cancels the linear term algebraically before taking absolute values. For x=theta R: A=sum n2..9 (n-1) Bdn x^n + MD theta^10(9-8theta)/(1-theta)^2. W=sum n1..9 n Bdn x^n + MD theta^10(10-9theta)/(1-theta)^2. E=sum n1..8 Ben x^n +128 theta^9/(1-theta). Then |F|<=(A+W E)/32 on that contour, since r<=1/32. All tails follow from the inherited full-disk MD and inverse-lapse sup128. No favorable sign of an uncertain coefficient is assumed. Log lapse L1=1; L2..5 from SIXTH.sixth_coefficients, L6 from SEVENTH, L7 from EIGHTH, L8 from NINTH. Independently valid same-family intervals. e0=1, en=-(1/n)sum(j=1..n) j Lj e(n-j). Absolute max over both engines. Keep min of new and previous valid full-F sups. Cauchy unknown>=10 clock tail remains M(theta)*(a/(theta R))^10/(1-a/(theta R)). Four arms: exa

## 20261027_COMPONENT_QUARTIC_CLOCK_ENCLOSURE

Same family, chart, clock and sealed scalar recurrence. Improve only separation coefficient majorant: B_n contains n^2||b_n||_(2n), so bhat_n=4^n*w_n/n^2. X_n=sumwall n^3|xi_n|<=lambda*w_n; conservatively xhat_n=2lambda*4^n*w_n/n^3 bounds affine graph slope. Set xhat_1=0 by exact first graph identity. Exact pulled separation yields D-D0 coefficientwise <=3[exp(bhat)*(1+xhat)-1], L<=3. This has no separate independent oscillator entry, but its induced effects remain inside w_n. Do not apply another Eulerian composition to pulled b. Compute finite positive series throughdegree4 and extract symmetric d4 bound. Replay aggregate recurrence and former d4 enclosure exactly; require new d4 domination. Reconstruct F4 using same archived L2/L3/d1..3 and exact symbolic identity. Replay parentF4 and full quartic clock certificate, then change only newF4abs in retained fourth-order term. Unknowntail staysdegree5; all four remainder gates and complete geometry/contraction/seed/radius baseline replay

## 20261027_DISTANCE24_CLOCK_READOUT

Prospective paired full-certificate readout after actual d6 and degree24 positive majorant audits. Baseline reproduces ACTUAL_SIXTH_CLOCK_CERTIFICATE both arm exactly, gain reset1. No gate, global radius, retained clock or inverse-lapse coefficient changes. Distance envelope retains actual d1..6 bounds and inherited generic d7..16. At17..24 take the smaller of positive-majorant coefficient and global Cauchy MD/R^n, both valid. Unknown distance begins25. Direct clock high-degree tail still begins10: finite distance terms(n-1)dn for n10..24; finite distance-inverse products n dn em for n+m>=10; unknown distance MD q25(24-23q)/(1-q)^2; its Euler product MD q25(25-24q)/(1-q)^2 times full inverse departure; and retained distance Euler polynomial times inverse tail JC q9/(1-q). Multiply complete sum by rate ceiling1/32. Compare minimum of new direct tail, old direct16 and old contour fallback. Distance gate similarly retains old bounds as fallback. Original scalar and opening gates unchanged

Actual eighth bulk algebra prerequisite, authorized by user's continue after completed seventh qualification and prospective next-test report. No new two-hour goal. Retain13:42:44UTC cutoff, Linux1CPU2GiB600s lease. Degree8 exact series of same original trace/mixed/scalar/radial equations. Import authenticated lower sources through7 by exact variable names. ## 20261027_EIGHTH_WALL_QUALIFICATION

Eighth moving-wall qualification. Ordinary eta coefficients, not factorial jets. The displacement begins at eta^2. Degree eight therefore requires first field jets through order six, second through four, third through two, and fourth background derivatives. No fourth perturbation jet can contribute before nine. The normal boost includes 1+v^2/2+3v^4/8; the squared factor includes 1+v^2+v^4. Fixed-coordinate time derivatives retain n*r, not the pulled-back derivative. Both wall orientations replay all prior coefficients through seven. The new oscillator divisor is 2+8r(8r+3h)E^2; the graph divisor is 8*sigma*E^2*r*(8r-h). Qualification checks exact Robin elimination, lower-only loads, complete normal boost convolution, omitted quartic boost detection, and all new jet locations. These are formal polynomial identities. New jets are independent symbols until separately differentiated and enclosed. No eighth coefficient, finite-amplitude trajectory, or physical fate follows from this qualif

## 20261027_FINITE_CONTOUR_CERTIFICATE

Finite-distance contour supremum, same analytic family and global disk. Authorized window ends 2026-10-27 11:38:31 UTC, no extension. For each fixed theta in (0,1) and Bn>=|dn| through9, let V=sum(n=1..9) Bn(theta R)^n + MD theta^10/(1-theta), W=sum(n=1..9) n Bn(theta R)^n + MD theta^10(10-9theta)/(1-theta)^2. Cauchy on the unchanged R disk supplies the unknown coefficients. Thus V bounds |D-D0| and W bounds |eta D'| on theta R. The same global inverse-lapse bound128 and r<=1/32 imply sup|F|<=(128W+V)/32. Take the minimum of this and the previously valid contour supremum. Subtract retained F terms only via Cauchy coefficient tail, not by claiming cancellation in the sup. Unknown>=10 tail remains MFtheta z^10/(1-z). Baseline exactly reconstructs the accepted contour-distance BOTH arm. Four fixed arms: baseline; extended contours only; finite supremum on original contours; finite supremum with extended contours. Original contours are 1/2,2/3,3/4,4/5,9/10,10/11,15/16. Extended additionall

## 20261027_FINITE_CONTOUR_CERTIFICATE_R1

Finite-distance contour supremum, same analytic family and global disk. Authorized window ends 2026-10-27 11:38:31 UTC, no extension. For each fixed theta in (0,1) and Bn>=|dn| through9, let V=sum(n=1..9) Bn(theta R)^n + MD theta^10/(1-theta), W=sum(n=1..9) n Bn(theta R)^n + MD theta^10(10-9theta)/(1-theta)^2. Cauchy on the unchanged R disk supplies the unknown coefficients. Thus V bounds |D-D0| and W bounds |eta D'| on theta R. The same global inverse-lapse bound128 and r<=1/32 imply sup|F|<=(128W+V)/32. Take the minimum of this and the previously valid contour supremum. Subtract retained F terms only via Cauchy coefficient tail, not by claiming cancellation in the sup. Unknown>=10 tail remains MFtheta z^10/(1-z). Baseline exactly reconstructs the accepted contour-distance BOTH arm. Four fixed arms: baseline; extended contours only; finite supremum on original contours; finite supremum with extended contours. Original contours are 1/2,2/3,3/4,4/5,9/10,10/11,15/16. Extended additionall

From exact Q2,Q3 and certified complex H<=3,T<=2,mPhi<=2: sp=3cn/n+64bm;sc=36sp/n+112cn/n^2+br; sg=(2/n+4/n^2)cn+12sp/n+bk. From Q3,Q6,Q7: F=sg+80sc/(dc n^2);rw=2cn/n+39xn/n^2+2bj+bo/2; g=(1/2+1/(d0 n^2))*rw+2sc/(d0 n), S=F+g. All equal previous source formulas at n2. Actual signs are bounded absolutely. Use SAME-row certified complex x,y,total and real Cv,Cg for each finite n and uniform tail n>=65. Existing derivative/continuation payments retained: xp=[Cm(sc+sp)+(Cg/n+9Cv/n^2)S/d0+rho(xS/6+sp/2)]/(1-1.5rho), yp=3xp+xS/3+sp;up=2xp+4yp+13xS/6+yS/3+9sp/rho; B=3up+cn+totalS;C=32768[(Cv/n)S+xn/4+bo]. Tail uses uniform inverse constants and n65 for decreasing explicit payments. Forcing lane retains old sp/sc/sg but matches remaining source transfers and inverse. Joint lane also matches sp/sc/sg.

## 20261027_MATCHED_METRIC_CERTIFICATE

Prospective exact metric anchor and harmonic continuation comparison. Baseline is sealed JOINT_SOURCE_CERTIFICATE joint lane, exact81row replay. ## 20261027_NINTH_CLOCK_ENCLOSURE

PROSPECTIVE CONTINGENCY. Eligible only within the existing two-hour window after independent qualification of the eighth-order parent and measured dominance of its unknown degree9-and-higher clock tail. No new launch after06:30:11UTC. Linux-only, exclusive1core,600second arithmetic cap, no ODE or sealed-parent changes. Same family, normalization, source map, global radius and full certificate gates. Independently reviewed moving-wall expansion: L8=p8_pulled+Hxi8+Hprime(xi2xi6+xi3xi5+xi4^2/2)+(Hsecond/2)(xi2^2xi4+xi2xi3^2)+Hthird xi2^4/24-v2v6-v3v5-v4^2/2-v2^4/4. The final boost term first appears here and must not be omitted. Hthird=-2(Tprime^2+T Tsecond)/3+2kE^2(Hprime-2H^2). For a= B_pulled-P_pulled-log a_background, use a1=-3p1, a2=c2-3p2-Hxi2, a3=c3-3p3-3u1xi2-Hxi3 with full Eulerian lower p2/p3. The fourth coefficient can instead use a4=c4-3p4_pulled+xi2 c2prime-Hxi4-Hprime xi2^2/2. This is equivalent because p4_pulled=p4_Eulerian+xi3u1+xi2u2; do not include those transports twice

## 20261027_NINTH_JET_QUALIFICATION

Ninth spatial-jet qualification under authorized cutoff17:10:42UTC. Same600second cap and exclusive1CPU2GiB Linux lease; no parent changes. Extend authenticated eighth derivative map with seventh-order field equations. Differentiate exactly the11 new wall inputs using polynomial derivative and separate coefficient-level Leibniz rule. Inputs: u7,g7prime,c7prime,u5prime, g5second,c5second,u3second,g3third,c3third,u1third,g1fourth. Here u1third is p1 fourth spatial derivative; g1fourth is f1 fifth derivative, because g1 itself is f1prime. Do not differentiate unsigned enclosure bounds. Authenticate prior eighth and seventh jets, fifth/seventh bulk records. Independent dictionary differentiation and RHS anchoring audit required. No field enclosure or amplitude claim until separately audited downstream work.

## 20261027_NINTH_REAL_SOURCE_ENCLOSURE

Actual unsigned ninth-distance coefficient on the same selected analytic family. Use audited ninth bulk/wall/jets, inherited unsigned eighth field bounds, and the existing finite-order9 inverse and scalar transfer exactly once. source_size=forcing_abs+9*Robin_abs; f9_bound=Cf9*size/81; f9prime_bound=Cf9*size/9. Reconstruct p9 using positive D9, then bound the metric integral and evaluate both moving endpoints with qualified jets. Do not differentiate unsigned field bounds; evaluate qualified jet polynomials. Ninth transport is coefficient of E*xi+Eprime*xi^2/2+Esecond*xi^3/6 +Ethird*xi^4/24. Unlike order8, E1 third spatial derivative contributes at order9 and must be retained. E0=1 has zero derivatives; fifth shift has degree>=10. Check polynomial convolution and omission sensitivity. The d9 enclosures from both inherited parameter engines must overlap; this is compatibility, not an independent accuracy estimate. Parameters and domain are checked against each engine's INITIAL record. N

## 20261027_POSITIVE_TAIL_FEASIBILITY

Bounded positive-majorant tail feasibility at fixed eta/R=1/16 and1/8, after independently verified actual seventh coefficient/full certificate. No actual eighth coefficient, no new field solve and no domain extension. Use the authenticated norm, seed s, route weights lambda,kappa, K and positive H_m,z_m,d_m through24. For all higher source orders use the already certified H_m <=(m-1)K^(m-1). Define the positive majorizing source Hhat(y)=sum_{m2..24}H_m*y^m+y*(K*y)^24*(24-23*K*y)/(1-K*y)^2. Its implicit solution zhat=s*t+Hhat(zhat) coefficientwise dominates the existing positive majorant and has the same first24 coefficients. P=sum_{n1..24}z_n*t^n. With fixed ystar=2*s*t, require ystar<=Y, K*ystar<1, P<=ystar, s*t+Hhat(ystar)<=ystar, L=Hhat'(ystar)<1. Then residual e=s*t+Hhat(P)-P is nonnegative by exact coefficient matching, and delta=e_upper/(1-L_upper) bounds zhat-P, provided P+delta<=ystar. All expressions use authenticated512-bit outward rational intervals; independent unrounded i

## 20261027_POSITIVE_TAIL_FULL_READOUT

POSITIVE UNRESOLVED TAIL: TWO-POINT COMPLETE READOUT Fixed q=eta/R in {1/16,1/8}. No new amplitude search or radius. Requires independent positive-tail R1 audit before launch. All parent complete certificates are replayed exactly at those two fixed points. Let D24,E24 be existing unsigned proper-distance and J-1 inverse-lapse coefficient bounds. Known F3..9 remain unchanged. Positive-tail packet provides T_D >= sum(n>=25)|d_n|a^n and U_D >= sum(n>=25)n|d_n|a^n. Jtail=JC*q^25/(1-q), Efull=sum(E24_n*a^n)+Jtail bounds J-1, not J. Clock unresolved numerator is bounded by sum(n=10..24)(n-1)D_n*a^n +sum(n,m<=24,n+m>=10)n*D_n*E_m*a^(n+m) +U_D*(1+Efull)+sum(n<=24)n*D_n*a^n*Jtail. Divide by32, then by original clock allowance10*a^2. The unknown-distance term safely overpays n-1 with n. Its product with full E includes unknown-distance times unknown-lapse exactly once. Known-distance times unknown-lapse is separate. No subtraction of two upper bounds is used. All coefficients and products are no

Thus unknown-D times unknown-J is counted once, no term lost. Report both inverse options separately. Require new candidate no worse. Preserve all old fallback minima and all five gates. No scan or tuning. Technical completion does not imply either amplitude passes. A failure is a sufficient-certificate limitation, not a physical insta

## 20261027_QUARTIC_BULK_QUALIFICATION

Prospective exact neutral quartic bulk-source qualification, no radial solve. Use ordinary eta powers, same authenticated general positive growth rate. Direct degree4 expansion of inherited S6 bulk equations with lower-order fields satisfying the qualified quadratic/cubic ODEs. Independently coded closed expressions must reproduce all eight source/trace/mixed outputs. Replay every lower degree1..3 row and background scalar/radial rows. Use c4=-L times the independently expanded trace polynomial, L=r/[4(4r-h)]. The mixed coefficient is analytically divided by its common r; Sp4=Hc4-qM4/(4r). Sc4 and Sg4 use Q3 exactly. Differentiate Sc4 under the lower ODE map and reduce its Bianchi residual modulo C3,C2,C1 and the background first integral; then substitute and clear the three fixed rational parameters C=r/[2(2r-h)], J=4r/[3(3r-h)], L. Nonzero remainder is qualification-inconclusive, not a physical incompatibility claim. Preserve all evidence and do not weaken the test after observation.

## 20261027_QUARTIC_CLOCK_ENCLOSURE

Same selected neutral growing family and lower-wall proper clock. Ordinary powers eta=epsilon exp(rt); log ell=eta+L2 eta^2+L3 eta^3+... . F=r eta D'/ell-r(D-D0). Exact polynomial identity F4=r[3d4-3d3+(1-2L2)d2+(-L3+L2-1/6)d1]. Lower wall graph begins at degree2, so velocity square enters log lapse only at degree4. Thus L3=p3(a)+H(a)xi3_a+p1'(a)xi2_a, available in authenticated cubic-affine wall data. Both representations retained, exact archived L2/F2/F3 replay. Inherited scalar recurrence W=s eta+K W^2/(1-KW)^2 implies w1=s,w2=Ks^2,w3=4K^2s^3,w4=18K^3s^4. Compute by finite exact recurrence and independently check closed forms. At real radial inner collar d>=1/2, Nagumo coefficient conversion pays4^n. U=(1+lambda+kappa)W(4eta) majorizes all fields/graphs conservatively. Exact pulled D integral has |b|<=U,|Xu|<=2U and L<=3; coefficientwise (exp(U)-1)+2Uexp(U)<=3U/(1-U), hence D-D0<=9U/(1-U). Use inherited harmonic coefficient bounds on p4 andxi4, archived actualxi2,H0,T0,k,E0 and H0'=−T0^2/3−kE0^2. This keeps the first induced-lapse boost correction atdegree4 with its negative sign. Exact F5/r=4d5−4d4+3d3(1/2−L2)+2d2(−L3+L2−1/6)+d1(−L4+L3+L2^2/2−L2/2+1/24). Symbolic exp inverse verifies all F1..F5. Keep sameF4enclosure, explicitB5|eta|^5; only unknownclocktailmoves todegree6. ## 20261027_QUINTIC_WALL_QUALIFICATION

Fifth-order moving-wall exact qualification, prepared before launch. Same selected neutral growing analytic family, original wall equations and original clock. No field solve or finite trajectory. Write shift=xi2 eta^2+xi3 eta^3+xi4 eta^4+xi5 eta^5, ordinary (not factorial) coefficients. Pull back background and perturbation fields by Taylor expansion to degree5; include xi2^2 p1''/2 in pwall, xi2^2 f1''/2 in deltaPhi, and xi2^2 f1'''/2 in radial scalar derivative. Background scalar degree5 term is Phi'' xi2 xi3. Fixed-time derivatives are taken before pullback, unlike total wall derivatives. The Lorentz factor gamma is 1+v^2/2 through degree5, since v begins at degree2 (the inherited code variable is called inverseboost). Graph uses inverseboost2=1+v^2. All equations are expanded as rational polynomials on independent wall jets and both orientations. Predeclared acceptance: coefficients0..4 equal authenticated quartic wall equations; fifth new rows have expected linear oscillator, sca

## 20261027_RETAINED_BOUND_ATTRIBUTION

Read-only source data reduction for archived rate-scaled F8,F9 term intervals. For each input engine separately, take absolute upper bound of each stored term interval. Sum all lower-product upper bounds; separate leading term. Leading/(leading+lower) is a share of this triangle BOUND, not of the true physical coefficient. Do not infer cancellation, sign or actual dominance. Normalize both by a^n/(10a^2), a=R/2, purely for comparison with the audited clock budget. Keep stored direct interval bound and adopted minimum separate: the triangle sum need not equal the producer's grouped interval evaluation. No deletion of a term, rescored acceptance gate, field solve or new radius. This determines whether generic leading d8/d9 bounds deserve the next test. Linux1CPU2GiB600seconds, unchanged campaign deadline13:42:44UTC.

Retain complete complex value,gradient,curvature response and full norm. Metric real anchor retains Cm*(sc+sp), but scalar part becomes (Cg/2+9Cv/4)*S/d0 from real |T|<=1,|Tprime|<=9,n>=2. This equals the old (11/4)*C0*S/d0 if Cv=Cg=C0 and thus does not mix in tightening of source coefficients. Oscillator endpoint uses Cv*S. All complex metric derivative costs remain as in COMPONENT_ROUTED_CERTIFICATE. Parent receives its own saved complex constants and unchanged real C0. Require exact all81 parent rows, selected constants and full certificate replay. Matched graph mass equal; bulk and oscillator masses nonincreasing. Certify both selected maps with all contraction,geometry,clock,scalar-tail g

## 20261027_SEVENTH_JET_QUALIFICATION

Seventh wall qualification passed and audited. This successor generates the eight required spatial jets from authenticated lower reduced equations. Selected background k=h^2 is required, with fixed r,h and trace factors. No differentiation of those constants. u_n=p_n', g_n=f_n'. u5 and g5prime are their reduced first-order equations. c5prime is the spatial derivative of qualified c5. u3prime,g3second,c3second are first, first,second spatial derivatives of u3,g3prime,c3 respectively. u1second and g1third are second derivatives of u1 and g1prime. Two exact polynomial differentiation routes must agree. Independent audit must reconstruct the saved derivatives and review the inherited RHS map. All arithmetic Linux, exclusive1CPU2GiB,600second cap; fixed deadline 2026-10-27T13:42:44UTC. No result-dependent changes to gates or regrouping.

## 20261027_SEVENTH_REAL_SOURCE_ENCLOSURE

Prospective unsigned actual seventh coefficient enclosure. Same neutral pure-growing analytic family and authenticated homogeneous domain. No ODE. Require successful seventh bulk, wall and spatial-jet qualifications and their independent audits; inherit actual sixth field bounds and its audit. New eight jets are evaluated as qualified expanded polynomials, with exact outward512-bit endpoint operations; audit uses unrounded rational endpoints with the same qualified polynomial ordering and strict containment. No numerical differentiation or fitted seventh profile. Constants r,h, trace factors are spatially fixed. The inherited background has k=h^2. Whole-domain lower fields and symmetric actual sixth fields feed seventh bulk sources. Positive trace, radial, oscillator and graph divisors required. At each endpoint chi6=(f6+T*xi6-o6)/d6_wall. Qualified seventh zero-new-field wall loads yield xi7 and the inherited Robin formula. With S=forcing_abs +7*Robin_abs, finite inverse yields f7<=Cf

## 20261027_SEVENTH_WALL_QUALIFICATION

Conditional seventh moving-wall algebra prerequisite; same selected family. Activate only after independently audited finite-inverse clock readout shows F7 alone exceeding the next clock allowance and nonclock gates passing. Extend ordinary degree-six Taylor pullback through degree7. Shift begins at order2. Third spatial derivatives of first-order fields first enter at order7: include p1'''=u1second, f1'''=g1second and g1'''=g1third, with the exact shift-cubed/6 factors. Add first jets at order5 and second jets at order3. These are independent symbolic variables until later enclosed. Background shift powers through3 remain sufficient; degree4 starts8. Boost powers v^4 also begin8, so 1+v^2/2 and 1+v^2 remain sufficient. Replay all inherited wall rows through6 for both orientations. Check the seventh linear oscillator/graph/scalar rows, exact Robin elimination and lower-only loads. Independently compute the seventh normal-boost term from v2..v5 and unboosted normal coefficients A0..A3.

## 20261027_SIXTH_WALL_QUALIFICATION

Prospective sixth moving-wall algebra prerequisite for an actual d6 bound in the same neutral growing family. Independent of the new sixth bulk calculation; neither alone certifies d6. Use ordinary degree-six Taylor pullback. Separate fixed-time derivatives from total wall derivatives. Shift starts at eta^2, so log background lapse requires H'' shift^3/6; background scalar pullback requires T'' shift^3/6; background scalar gradient requires T''' shift^3/6. First and second spatial jets of perturbations are retained only to orders contributing through degree six. New jets are independent algebraic variables in this test, not yet supplied numerical bounds. Lorentz factors truncate to 1+v^2/2 and 1+v^2 because v starts at order2; v^4 starts at order8. Require exact equality with every authenticated degree0..5 wall row, all sixth linear new-variable operators for both orientations, elimination to Robin load, lower-only loads, and an explicit independent degree-six normal-boost correction.

## 20261027_UNIFORM_POSITIVE_TAIL_BRACKET

Uniform scaled positive-tail sufficient certificate, fixed16 bisections. For any nonnegative-coefficient remainder T(a)=sum(n>=25)c_n*a^n, 0<a<=a*=R/2 implies T(a)<=(a/a*)^25*T(a*). Same for its Euler-weighted remainder and each scalar harmonic-weighted majorant. Use independently qualified endpoint bounds at a*=R/2 for distance, Euler distance, scalar, Euler scalar, and inverse-lapse tails. These upper bounds need not be sharp. Positivity belongs to underlying majorant series, not floating-point samples or apparent empirical scaling. Combine scaled tails with unchanged finite24 arrays and F3..9, identical clock partition and all five gates. No Cauchy fallback min is needed for this standalone sufficient certificate. At a* require exact replay of the independently audited direct endpoint selected values and every gate. At R/4 use this uniform bound, not an asserted replay of the sharper separately evaluated quarter-point tail. Require it passes and R/2 fails. All normalized non-opening
